# FLGB-16 — Assets Build Export Performance and Automated Testing

Canonical overlay: Functional LLM + Game Builder 10MB execution atlas
Masterplan relationship: depth-only; VOL-000..420 breadth freeze preserved
Implementation authority: none
Implementation signed: false
Independent verification signed: false

## Plane objective
Close the specification surface for Assets Build Export Performance and Automated Testing while preserving bounded execution, adversarial quality improvement, copyright/rights governance and exact evidence requirements.

## FLGB-16-00001 — asset import / contract / unit proof
**Outcome.** asset import must be implementable as a finite contract transaction under cold start, with explicit ownership, versioned contracts, deterministic IDs, project isolation, cancellation and typed terminal states.

**Implementation map.** Bind `skeleton/game/build/asset_import.py` to durable OperationEnvelope/ExecutionContext semantics plus project/scene/artifact identities. Any generated code, asset, narrative, rule or build product remains a candidate until validation and promotion succeed.

**Adversarial quality rule.** When the rival forge applies, proposer and challenger operate with equal bounded authority; verifier is independent of both and owns no production write authority. Forge-100/1000/10000 increase evaluation effort, not privilege. Stop on budget/deadline/cancellation/convergence or repeated no-gain hypotheses.

**Rights rule.** External gaming sources are evidence/reference inputs only. Track source/license/terms/provenance, forbid unauthorized copying, detect suspicious similarity, require clean-room re-expression for influenced designs, and quarantine unclear assets. Rights failure is non-compensable.

**Acceptance.** unit proof must prove the intended result plus cold start, replay stability, rollback or compensation, exact artifact lineage and rights-policy behavior. Whole-project changes additionally verify long-form canon, dependency graph integrity and editor/runtime/export agreement.

**Failure injection.** Exercise corrupt artifacts, stale rivals, contradictory canon, engine incompatibility, failed export, denied permission, missing license evidence, resource exhaustion and partial commits. Recovery must never silently promote an unverified candidate.

**Metrics.** Measure verified quality delta, regressions caught, false promotions prevented, build reproducibility, playtest success, resource budgets, rights quarantines, rollback success and unresolved gap count. Quality scores cannot override non-compensable gates.

**Closure state.** specification=defined; implementation_signed=false; independent_verification_signed=false; completion_claim=false. Exact-head executable evidence is mandatory.
